    Examples as flows¶

    Every examples/* folder, the flow that reproduces it in Flow Studio (Open... -> Examples) and the outcome of running that flow. Generated by python -m pinneapple_flow example-flows doc from pinneapple_flow/flows/examples/ (the flows), inventory.json (statuses and reasons) and measured.json (written by example-flows run: every number below was measured by a run of the flow, none is typed in). tests/test_flow_examples.py fails when a folder has neither a flow nor a reason.

    Statuses: runs (the flow runs in its full configuration), runs-quick (it runs in its documented quick configuration, meta.quick of the flow; the full one is too long for this machine), needs X (a tool that is not installed here), not_convertible (the reason says why).

    status examples
    needs 7
    no flow 2
    not yet run 2
    not_convertible 20
    runs 42
    runs-quick 15
    example domain flow(s) status same numbers notes
    architectures neural architectures architectures/00_registry_tour
    architectures/01_list_models
    architectures/30_rom_pod_dmd
    architectures/60_new_architectures_demo    		 runs 00_registry_tour, 01_list_models and 60_new_architectures_demo are flows of architectures.registry + architectures.forward (output shapes as in the scripts; the GraphBatch GNN / MeshGraphNet parts and the non-registry layers are left out); 30_rom_pod_dmd is architectures.pod_dmd (the data generator moved to rom.toy_systems; POD and rollout MSE equal the script's). 02-05 are per-class demos with training loops in the scripts, 10 (FNO), 20 (inverse ODE PINN) and 40 (GNN) train in the scripts, and 50 imports pinneapple_models.transformers.transformer, which does not exist
    arena_pipelines benchmarks arena_pipelines/00_list_datasets
    arena_pipelines/01_physics_benchmark
    arena_pipelines/02_timeseries_benchmark
    arena_pipelines/04_arena_from_yaml    		 runs-quick flows for 00 (dataset registry: 24 / 24 load), 01 (PhysicsBenchmarkSpec: Burgers, heat, inverse heat, Kovasznay), 02 (TimeSeriesBenchmarkSpec x 4) and 04 (pinneapple_arena.Arena on the built-in Kovasznay config; configs/*.yaml through config_file); 05's config dicts run through the same arena.run node. 03, 06 and 08 hold custom training or ArenaProblem subclasses; 07 passes Python lambdas to define_problem; 09's benchmark_dataset returns the problem, not results. Nothing committed to compare
    autolab design autolab/self_optimizing_engineer runs 6 / 6 match self_optimizing_engineer: autolab.board_cooling (#867) + examples.flatten, compared with the committed _out/self_optimizing_engineer/summary.json (heat-sink mass, fan power, pessimistic junction temperatures)
    autolab_aero aerodynamics autolab_aero/autolab_aero runs-quick autolab.aerodynamicist (#881): the full configuration runs the OpenFOAM campaign (20 simpleFoam runs, hours) and is compared with _out/summary.json (drag improvement); the quick configuration shows the dashboard of the recorded session, which recomputes nothing and so compares nothing
    autolab_aero_agent aerodynamics - not_convertible agent_mission.py drives pinneapple_autolab.agent.run_agent with a ReplaySimulator over stage 1's ledger and two refusal cases, arranged in the script; no node wraps the replayed agent session (autolab.aerodynamicist runs the agent on live OpenFOAM instead, see autolab_aero), and an LLM provider is optional
    autolab_digital_twin digital twin autolab_digital_twin/twin_knows_when_wrong runs 2 / 2 match twin_knows_when_wrong: autolab.pipe_twin (#869) + examples.flatten, compared with the committed _out/summary.json (diagnosis accuracy, cases with a false alarm)
    autolab_rover vehicles autolab_rover/rover_learns_planet runs 12 / 12 match rover_learns_planet: rover.learn_planet (#871), compared with results/summary.json (learned soil-parameter errors, multibody distance, energy, slip and sinkage of routes A and B)
    benchmark benchmarks benchmark/01_equal_budget runs-quick 01_equal_budget: pp.benchmark.run (benchmark.equal_budget), quick = the script's smoke sizes; nothing committed to compare
    benchmark_suite benchmarks benchmark_suite/02_arena_from_yaml
    benchmark_suite/12_physics_benchmark_suite
    benchmark_suite/13_transfer_meta_benchmark    		 runs-quick flows for 02 (run_arena_experiment on configs/experiment_burgers_1d.yaml: the library's rel_l2 / r2 on the reference points come out degenerate, about 1e12), 12 (PINNArenaBenchmark through benchmark.arena with the registry's bench_* models: the script's VanillaPINN_S... names are no longer registered) and 13 (transfer + meta pipelines, quick = --fast; the Burgers reference solver overflows for nu = 0.005 / pi). 12 and 13 import pinneapple_arena.benchmark / transfer_benchmark / meta_benchmark, now in pinneapple_tools.benchmark_suite. Not convertible: 01, 04, 07 (genetic) and 08 import pinneapple_arena.runner / bundle / registry or pinneapple_digital_twin modules that no longer exist; 09 calls run_full_pipeline, broken in the library (ThroughputMonitor(log_every=...), missing pinneapple_environment); 03, 05, 06, 07_xtfc, 10 and 14-17 write their losses, loops or solvers in the scripts; 11 builds its ProblemSpec in script code around Arena.from_spec. Nothing committed to compare
    biology biology - not_convertible four independent studies (identifiability with exact ODE sensitivities, PK/PD with MCMC, an eikonal PINN of 20,000 steps on an Aliev-Panfilov map, hours of Windkessel-coupled pimpleFoam); no flow node wraps them yet
    black_hole_weather astrophysics black_hole_weather/accretion_run runs 1 / 1 match the flow is one run of the resolution study (90 x 45, seed 0) and its statistics; the 12-run convergence study (2 h per 256 x 128 run), train_forecaster.py, skill.py and evaluate.py (the blackhole_riaf_density_256 Hub dataset; huggingface.co is not reachable from this machine) are not flows
    bricks design bricks/self_modifying_bricks runs-quick 15 / 15 match compared with the committed _out/summary.json (--full: 400 evaluations x 5 seeds); the quick configuration runs seed 0 of each search at the full budget (the seeds are independent runs) and the physics checks. The viewer, GIF / MP4, Blender renders and building instructions of the script are not nodes
    calculix_cantilever structures calculix_cantilever/beam runs 4 / 4 match no script in the folder: the flow solves the committed beam.inp with ccx (installed here from apt calculix-ccx) and compares with the committed beam.frd
    chain_of_whys design reasoning chain_of_whys/roof runs 1 / 1 match chain_of_whys/roof is the roof case (the bundled flow reason_roof of #858: reason.* nodes), compared with the committed _out/roof/chain.json; the footbridge, power_board and pumped_main cases have no flow of their own yet
    condensation_falsification heat transfer - a falsification campaign: dozens of pre-registered studies run in subprocesses (minutes to an hour each, up to 7.6 GB), judged by their kill criteria in run.py; the condensation itself is the Flow node condense.package_thermal (examples/physics_condensation)
    control control engineering control/cartpole
    control/submarine_depth    		 runs submarine_depth: naval.submarine_depth runs the study of the script (open loop, the two command architectures with their closed-loop eigenvalues, placed gains). The script's optimisation-based tuning and Monte Carlo robustness are in the library (pinneapple_systems.control.tune_by_optimization, robustness) but not in the node, to keep it under a few seconds. cartpole: control.cartpole runs the whole study of cartpole_swing_up.py (linearisation, LQR, swing-up, push) with the same numbers; control.linear_analysis shows the upright A matrix's unstable pole.
    core_primitives core - not_convertible demonstrates the pinneapple_core Field / FunctionField API with hand-written training loops; the same physics is the poisson_2d flow (equations + train.pinn + validate)
    data_api core - not_convertible demonstrates the pinneapple_core.data loaders with hand-written loops; the same three problems are equations + train.pinn, surrogate.train and inverse.discover flows
    data_pipeline data - not_convertible API demos of pinneapple_data that write Zarr stores and stream them (loaders, prefetch threads, GPU pinning); 05 needs cadquery; nothing is computed or committed
    design_from_intent design design_from_intent/design_from_intent runs 21 / 30 match the loop's full check needs NVIDIA Newton and the bracket's CalculiX + gmsh; without Newton that verification does not run
    dynamics_toolkit dynamical systems - not_convertible pinneapple_analysis.dynamics has no flow nodes yet (its coverage reason in pinneapple_flow/coverage.py); the script composes library calls only: pp.solve (PINN), survival_from_solution, DMD, ObservationScheduler, BuckinghamPiAnalyzer / CurveCollapseAnalyzer and EarlyWarningAnalyzer

    near_random_forecasting/random_walk_case    		 runs-quick 30 / 36 match one flow per case shown here (random walk, Lorenz-63); every other case of presets.CASES is the same forecast_lab.case node with another case name (usd_eur_log_returns, sunspots_yearly and the youtube cases download their data); render_visuals.py (Blender renders of the committed cases) is not a node yet. In random_walk the baselines match; the two learned models (scikit-learn HistGradientBoosting, torch MLP) differ by 0.1-3 % on the test RMSE, and the committed results do not record the scikit-learn / torch versions (here scikit-learn 1.9.1)
    non_hermitian non-Hermitian physics non_hermitian/ep_tuning runs 9 / 9 match ep_laws / ep_numeric / hypothesis_map / ep_figure wrap pinneapple_physics.non_hermitian (coupling_classes moved from the script into ep_tuning.py, the script prints the same output); compared with docs/core_concepts/non_hermitian.md (EP-law degree, kappa_N^2 and EP order for N = 2-5)
    numerical_solvers numerical solvers numerical_solvers/12_molecular_dynamics_lj_gas
    numerical_solvers/13_shallow_water_dam_break    		 runs flows for 12 (LJ molecular dynamics) and 13 (shallow water, without the Twin3D server); nothing committed to compare. 01-06 and 08-11 import modules that no longer exist (pinneapple_solvers.registry, .fem, .fvm, .hilbert_huang, cfd_pipeline, pinneapple_train.preprocess); 07 (SPH) builds isph / dfsph through a registry the shim does not populate
    nvidia NVIDIA stack - needs usd-core (pxr), nvidia-physicsnemo and usd-brep 01, 07, 12-14 and 16-18 write USD stages (usd-core); 02, 07-11, 16 and 18 train NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo models; 05, 10 and 14-16 build B-rep geometry with usd-brep: none of the three is installed here. 03 (robot sim), 04 (world model physics video) and 06 (model services) run on PINNeAPPle alone, but each study is arranged in its run.py main() (datasets, training, verdict rates) with no library entry point a node could call
    openfoam_campaign CFD openfoam_campaign/campaign runs no committed numbers (_out/results is not committed); the flow reports each run's solver health and profiles. OpenFOAM v1912 from apt
    pde_environment PINN pde_environment/07_tagged_presets_real_geometry runs-quick 07_tagged_presets_real_geometry is a flow (pde_env.tagged_preset: build_tag_batch + per_tag_losses, moved from the script into presets.tag_geometry, + solve_pde); nothing committed to compare, the before-training losses equal the script's. 01, 02 and 03_ns2d evaluate compile_problem once on a batch the script samples and assembles by hand; 03_elasticity3d and 04_heat3d need trimesh and STL files; 05_kepler and 06_space_debris train with a nondimensionalisation wrapper and a loop written in the script
    physics_audit physics audit - not_convertible the case study (Churchill truth, model lambdas, profiles, 160-case stream) is built in the script; PhysicsEnsemble / PhysicsAwareRouter have no case generator to call
    physics_check validation physics_check/fail_and_pass runs 3 / 3 match the torch/ fixtures (a zoo model) and generate.py (regenerates the fixtures with OpenFOAM) are not flows; workflow.yml is a GitHub Action template
    physics_condensation heat transfer physics_condensation/hierarchy_64 runs 1 / 1 match hierarchy_64: condense.package_thermal at 64 x 64 blocks, m = 12, compared with the committed scaling point (quick: 16 x 16). The other studies of run_benchmark.py (accuracy against modes, refinement, Keff, learned blocks, transient, meshless, exception patches) are pinneapple_condense.benchmark functions that run for minutes in subprocesses and are measured by the script.
    physics_db data - needs earthaccess + NASA Earthdata login / network 00-02 query NASA Earthdata or NASA POWER; 03-06 write shards with the private PhysicalDatasetBuilder._write_upd from a dataset built in the script
    physics_ensemble ensembles - not_convertible the expert training loops, ring-graph adapters, ParametricPINN and FD schemes are in the scripts (docs/dev/LIBRARY_CONSOLIDATION.md lists them for a later pass); multiscale_turbulence takes hours
    physics_learning_verification verification physics_learning_verification/detection runs 4 / 4 match detection: analysis.physics_learning running the detection study (seconds), compared with the committed results/detection.json. The real-model study (POD-GPR surrogates, PINNs trained for minutes) is measured by run_study.py; single surrogates can be checked with the same node.
    physics_module hybrid models physics_module/01_coarse_fem_plus_neural_correction runs 2 / 2 match compared with the numbers of docs/core_concepts/model.md
    physics_optimizer optimization physics_optimizer/01_constrained_shape_six_methods runs 12 / 12 match compared with the table of docs/core_concepts/physics_optimizer.md
    physics_query heat transfer - the demo's numbers are statistics over 5,115 queries compared with three fine finite-volume solves (about 10 minutes, run_demo.py); the engine itself is the Flow node query.package, which answers a list of queries on a package with value, uncertainty, method and whether it was read or predicted
    pinn_solver PINN pinn_solver/01_symbolic_loss_basic
    pinn_solver/02_compiler_poisson_2d
    pinn_solver/03_compiler_burgers_1d
    pinn_solver/04_factory_inverse_parameter_heat_1d
    pinn_solver/06_compiler_wave_1d    		 runs-quick 01 is a flow (pinn_solver.factory_loss: the PINNFactory loss, as the script); 02 Poisson, 03 Burgers, 06 wave and 04 inverse heat are flows of equations.system + train.pinn / inverse.discover + validate.compare (the same problem and error metric, trained by train.pinn instead of the script's compile_problem / PINNFactory loop); nothing committed to compare, and the quick configurations are far from converged. 03_symbolic_pde_hard_bc (hard-BC ansatz and loop), 05_upd_dataset (synthetic Zarr and loop), 06_domino (residuals and BCs), 06_open_meteo (three training pipelines, network) and 07_interface_bc (two-region batch and loop) hold their method in the scripts
    pinneapple_and_physicsnemo PINN - not_convertible pinneapple/pinneapple.py writes its own sampling, Adam loop, test metrics, PIELM gradient wrapper and output adapter (about 200 lines) around PINNFactory, so no library call reproduces the committed JSON; physicsnemo/ and both/ need NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo-Sym, which is not installed
    pinneapple_benchmark benchmarks - not_convertible two hand-written PINN benchmarks (760 lines of sampling, residuals and training loops around the architecture classes); nothing committed
    pinneapple_cosim co-simulation - not_convertible imports pinneapple_cosim, which no longer exists (moved to pinneapple_systems.cosimulation without a shim), and trains in the script
    potential_co2 thermodynamics potential_co2/surrogate_study runs-quick surrogate_study: potential.co2_surrogates at one seed and a reduced budget (no comparison with the committed numbers, which are means over five seeds at 3000 and 9000 steps); the full study (5 seeds x 3 configurations, about 1.5 h on CPU) runs as 01_run_study.py and 02_report.py builds the figures, report and manifest.
    problem_designer design problem_designer/03_offline_spec_to_report runs 03_offline_spec_to_report: nothing numeric committed; the flow writes the same report as the script (5 plan steps, no validator warnings). 01 and 02 call Gemini over the network; 04 and 05 use a mock LLM provider defined in the scripts
    proof_graph validation proof_graph/pt_chain_no_go runs 4 / 4 match status counts compared with the committed report docs/assets/proof_graph/pt_chain_no_go_report.md
    qualitative design qualitative/03_ahmed_slant runs 03_ahmed_slant: nothing numeric committed; the flow reproduces the guide's reading (docs/core_concepts/qualitative_preview.md): right direction at 12.5 and 40 degrees, wrong at 25, 30 and 35 with low confidence (direction agreement 0.4, rank correlation -0.60, the same as the script). 01 and 02 build their variants (heat sinks, beams) in the scripts
    quickstart getting started - not_convertible the thirteen recipes are a guided tour, not a study. 01 prints what the installed package knows (pp.info(), the preset and zoo listings) and computes nothing to compare; 02, 04, 06, 08, 10, 11, 12 and 13 train inside the script with hand-written loops over pp.solve / the trainer, and 12 writes runs/text_to_twin through the problem-design agent. A flow node wraps one library call with a comparable result, which would misrepresent a tour whose point is the printed sequence. The page the tour powers is still checked: the hub snippet example:examples/quickstart loads its experiment.json through pinneapple_lab.manifest and runs
    reproductions physics-informed ML - not_convertible paper reproductions under the protocol of pinneapple_analysis.reproduction: each folder freezes a paper's quoted targets in TARGETS.md before any run, then runs many seeds (up to 1000) of the paper's settings and scores them offline against those targets. A flow node wraps one library call with a comparable result; a seed campaign scored against frozen quotes is not one call, and a one-seed flow would misstate what the folder measures. The solvers it uses are library calls (xtfc_general.solve_xtfc) and the folder's page is checked: the hub snippets example:examples/reproductions and example:examples/reproductions/xtfc_schiassi2021 load their experiment.json through pinneapple_lab.manifest and run
    rigid_body multibody rigid_body/newton_quickstart runs NVIDIA Newton 1.7 (warp, CPU) installed with pip here

    From a description (planner)¶

    Each example with a flow and the planner capabilities that build it from text (pinneapple_flow.planner.extensions): first when the heuristic planner, given the capability's title as the description, proposes that capability first; the examples without a capability are where the planner falls short (it proposes generic flows only).
